[u0037 | 00:02:12.560-00:02:13.460 | DM] So
[u0038 | 00:02:14.840-00:02:18.529 | DM] Lastlight Falls in the fey realm of Amberglow
[u0039 | 00:02:18.930-00:02:28.240 | DM] is hidden among rocky cliffs, a place of calm where water cascades from the rocks above to clear—to a clear pool below, the long shadows
[u0040 | 00:02:28.530-00:02:31.260 | DM] of sunset spilling across the water.
[u0041 | 00:02:31.920-00:02:36.819 | DM] the last light of the day catching the spray and turning it to a glittering rainbow
[u0042 | 00:02:38.370-00:02:45.169 | DM] in the empty, desolate realm of Amberglow. This remains one of the few places of color and safety
[u0043 | 00:02:47.170-00:02:52.679 | DM] as a group of travelers gather here, some reunited after long separation, and others newly met
[u0044 | 00:02:53.090-00:02:58.790 | DM] the Cloudspinner feels their presence even from her prison far away, and she drifts in thought.
[u0045 | 00:03:00.200-00:03:03.360 | DM] She remembers the days of plenty in Amberglow
[u0046 | 00:03:03.600-00:03:10.079 | DM] when she ruled as the Queen of Sunsets, when the glorious colors of the evening sun turned the sky and the grass
[u0047 | 00:03:10.110-00:03:13.320 | DM] and the water and the rocks to ever changing paintings
[u0048 | 00:03:13.540-00:03:17.510 | DM] when she spun threads of magic and color from the sky.
[u0049 | 00:03:17.600-00:03:21.399 | DM] When her court dazzled any who came with her beauty and elegance.
[u0050 | 00:03:22.200-00:03:28.389 | DM] she remembers the fateful day. She was tricked, bound, and captive. Her tools stolen, her life constrained
[u0051 | 00:03:28.530-00:03:31.450 | DM] Amberglow slowly falling into ruin.
[u0052 | 00:03:32.360-00:03:37.140 | DM] She remembers how she could feel the footsteps of decay, a constant reminder of her fate
[u0053 | 00:03:37.360-00:03:40.090 | DM] as the color ran out from Amberglow.
[u0054 | 00:03:40.410-00:03:44.100 | DM] her magnificent Cloud Palace fell into disarray.
[u0055 | 00:03:44.410-00:03:48.920 | DM] Her court and servants fled or lost, save the few who hung on
[u0056 | 00:03:49.160-00:03:51.879 | DM] in desperation or fear or confusion.
[u0057 | 00:03:51.930-00:03:55.560 | DM] Slowly turning as pale and colorless as the land itself.
[u0058 | 00:03:57.450-00:04:04.059 | DM] she remembers the darkness that crept over her realm, the cursed ones, the hags and tricksters and malicious spirits
[u0059 | 00:04:04.230-00:04:06.029 | DM] of the Unseelie Court
[u0060 | 00:04:06.310-00:04:10.590 | DM] who came after she was gone who conquered and claimed Amberglow as their own.
[u0061 | 00:04:11.180-00:04:13.830 | DM] who filled the realm with darkness and despair.
[u0062 | 00:04:15.190-00:04:21.789 | DM] She remembers how they hunted down her remaining subjects, and how they desecrated the places that had once held such beauty and light.
[u0063 | 00:04:22.750-00:04:30.380 | DM] She also remembers the resistance, the brave fey and other creatures who fought back, who formed secret alliances and underground networks.
[u0064 | 00:04:30.480-00:04:34.569 | DM] who kept the spark of hope alive even in Amberglow's darkest days.
[u0065 | 00:04:34.900-00:04:37.880 | DM] Typhina, the protector of the Heartwood Grove.
[u0066 | 00:04:38.090-00:04:41.770 | DM] Lirien the Lady of the Lake, who drained the River of Time
[u0067 | 00:04:42.240-00:04:48.810 | DM] Bellator the Changeling King, who sacrificed himself to hide the ruins of the Cloud Palace from those that would mean harm.
[u0068 | 00:04:49.970-00:04:57.210 | DM] So she watches and waits, and she hopes for the day when she will be free, and when Amberglow will once again be a realm of beauty and wonder.
[u0069 | 00:04:57.740-00:05:03.479 | DM] Until then she will continue to dream and hold on to the memory of what once was and what could be again.
[u0070 | 00:05:04.880-00:05:13.559 | DM] And as our story fades from the Cloudspinner and turns now to the travelers, at Lastlight Falls you may yet have a small role to play in the story of Amberglow.

[u2811 | 03:12:59.500-03:13:02.769 | Caulaus] being appointed commander of the third Sylvan legion.
[u2812 | 03:13:06.230-03:13:07.060 | Caulaus] I
[u2813 | 03:13:07.940-03:13:11.879 | Caulaus] swore allegiance personally to the Emperor. I remember the
[u2814 | 03:13:12.380-03:13:15.259 | Caulaus] the place and the ceremony like it was yesterday
[u2815 | 03:13:15.420-03:13:17.260 | Caulaus] Emperor Helea.
[u2816 | 03:13:17.480-03:13:19.130 | Caulaus] a blond woman.
[u2817 | 03:13:19.530-03:13:21.399 | Caulaus] She was very striking.
[u2818 | 03:13:23.850-03:13:30.140 | Caulaus] wearing the robes of the ceremonial robes of gold and a golden crown with a diamond set in it.
[u2819 | 03:13:31.420-03:13:32.369 | Caulaus] and
[u2820 | 03:13:33.030-03:13:38.100 | Caulaus] I gave my oath to her, she holding a powerful scepter.
[u2821 | 03:13:38.460-03:13:39.580 | Caulaus] Now
[u2822 | 03:13:40.290-03:13:41.440 | Caulaus] she was
[u2823 | 03:13:41.770-03:13:45.389 | Caulaus] kind in private, although in public
[u2824 | 03:13:45.730-03:13:47.910 | Caulaus] she always seemed quite fearsome.
[u2825 | 03:13:48.530-03:13:50.979 | Caulaus] What she said to me I still remember.
[u2826 | 03:13:51.110-03:13:55.510 | Caulaus] she said, while your oath is to me your obligation is to the ideal of Drankor
[u2827 | 03:13:55.580-03:14:00.119 | Caulaus] a haven of safety and peace from the darkness for all humankind.
[u2828 | 03:14:00.520-03:14:03.300 | Caulaus] Remember that, and your command will be a great one.
[u2829 | 03:14:04.580-03:14:06.900 | Caulaus] I like to think that I did.
[u2830 | 03:14:07.140-03:14:09.419 | Caulaus] although I admit I cannot remember.

[u2833 | 03:14:14.530-03:14:16.329 | Caulaus] fighting in a forest

[u2836 | 03:14:26.980-03:14:30.089 | Caulaus] mages firing their spells at the enemy.

[u2838 | 03:14:33.560-03:14:40.349 | Caulaus] being warned by the elves of a danger of an army of giants coming from the mountains.
[u2839 | 03:14:41.950-03:14:47.059 | Caulaus] The battle was fierce, but I remember clearly the face of this frost giant I defeated.

[u2842 | 03:14:52.270-03:14:54.929 | Caulaus] his head nearly as big as myself.
[u2843 | 03:14:55.360-03:14:57.529 | Caulaus] his eyes angry and clouded
[u2844 | 03:14:57.850-03:15:02.350 | Caulaus] as I avoided his axe and struck the killing blow.
